Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. Hi... thank you so much for seeing me today. Um, I hope I'm answering this right, but my manager actually suggested I come in. I really want to do whatever is best, you know? I'm usually a very good waitress, I promise, but... well, I guess I've been having a few little mix-ups at work recently. 

I feel terrible about it because I think it kind of scared everyone. I just... I started sort of freezing up right in the middle of a busy service. Or I'd start doing things out of order?
